### Q1. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The line, that is coplanar to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>We know that two lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></math> are coplanar if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Let us verify this option.</p><p>The given lines are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math> xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" ### Q2. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math> parallel to the line of the shortest distance between the lines<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>The direction ratio of line parallel to the line of the shortest distance between the lines<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now given line passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>And having direction ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>,</p><p>So, the equation is given by, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Let the line intersect the plane at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> lies on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, the distance of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> will be, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q3. JEE Main 2023 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

<p>Let the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></math> be coplanar. If the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is nearest to the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>a</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>b</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>c</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>14</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p>The lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></math> be coplanar,</p><p>Now direction ratio of line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced><mn>7</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced><mn>5</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> lies on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math></p><p>Now solving above two equation we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>39</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>37</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>37</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>And <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>37</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>143</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>111</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>32</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>32</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>37</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and given point on line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> are coplanar,</p><p>So, by coplanar condition we get,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>32</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>37</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>87</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p>Now let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is nearest to point<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d51380dc-d716-445f-badc-b98f33484c64.png" style="width: 300px; height: 221px;" /></p><p>So, by perpendicular condition we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>14</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>a</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>b</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>c</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q4. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8477;</mi></math> meet the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math>. If the angle between the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> and the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><msqrt><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>14</mn></mfrac></msqrt></mfenced></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#947;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **11**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The given equations are,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>So, Vector parallel to line<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>:</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p>Normal vector to plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p>Angle between plane &amp; line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math>. Then angle between the line and normal to the plane will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></math>.
Then, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>90</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>&#183;</mo><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>But this point lies on the plane :</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math></p><p>Hence this is the required answer.</p>

</details>

### Q5. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let the line passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> meet the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>. Then the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> measured parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>61</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>189</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>31</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p>The line passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>,</p><p>So, equation of line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Now let point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> lies on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>R</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now let line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mi>R</mi></math> be :<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math> as it is parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> and passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e45b630c-53ea-4fcd-b4bf-b67d221457b6.png" style="width: 250px; height: 175px;" /></p><p>So, the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>:</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi></math> lies on plane : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p>So, distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q6. JEE Main 2023 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the point of intersection of the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>. If the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>q</mi></math> are the roots of the equation

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the point of intersection of the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Now let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Say, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Since point lie on the plane also,</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the point will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now finding the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>18</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>144</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>78</mn><mn>13</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p>Equation with roots <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>q</mi></math> will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>q</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>q</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>72</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q7. JEE Main 2023 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>Z</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>10</mn></math>, let the angle between the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math> If the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>32</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>85</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>48</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>32</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>Z</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>10</mn></math>, let the angle between the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p>Now rewriting the equation of line we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Now let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p>Now we know that, </p><p>Angle between plane and line is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#183;</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>Z</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>So, the equation of plane will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now given, distance of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from plane is<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>|</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>40</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q8. JEE Main 2023 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the equation of the plane containing the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and perpendicular to the plane<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>22</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>22</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>The equation of the plane containing the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and perpendicular to the plane<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, </p><p>Now let equation of required plane be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></math></p><p>So, the normal vector is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>And normal of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> will be, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now given they are perpendicular, so by perpendicular condition we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, equation of required plane will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mn>27</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>30</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>25</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>27</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>30</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Now comparing with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q9. JEE Main 2023 (01 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `3`

The point of intersection <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the line joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> divides the line segment <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> internally in the ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>|</mo></math> are coprime) are the direction ratios of the perpendicular from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to _____ .

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **10**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p>Plane: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now equation of line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>10</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Any point on line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now using perpendicular condition to find the point of intersection of line and plane, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>So, point of intersection will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi></math></p><p>Now any point on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/6cb6575d-50c4-4bd9-98db-e57b68da818c-9896844.png" style="width: 400px; height: 166px;" /></p><p>Now direction ratio of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now again using the perpendicular condition we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>14</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>42</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>130</mn></mrow><mn>42</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>85</mn><mn>42</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Now on simplifying the Direction ratios we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>42</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>130</mn></mrow><mn>42</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>85</mn><mn>42</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now on comparing with  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q10. JEE Main 2023 (31 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `3`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> be the angle between the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math>. Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> be the line that meets <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and makes an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> with the normal of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>cot</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to _____ .

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **9**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> be the angle between the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math>, so</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>And, </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/7efd81a5-32ca-4074-b168-50e6de8bd946-9896268.png" style="width: 300px; height: 270px;" /></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>cot</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q11. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane $2 x-y+z=4$ intersects the line segment joining the points $\mathrm{A}(\mathrm{a},-2,4)$ and $\mathrm{B}(2, \mathrm{~b},-3)$ at the point $\mathrm{C}$ in the ratio $2: 1$ and the distance of the point $\mathrm{C}$ from the origin is $\sqrt{5}$. If $a b&lt;0$ and $\mathrm{P}$ is the point $(\mathrm{a}-\mathrm{b}, \mathrm{b}, 2 \mathrm{~b}-\mathrm{a})$ then $\mathrm{CP}^2$ is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>17</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>16</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>73</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>97</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>17</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given points are:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> divides the line segment joining points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> in the ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, so</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> lies on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, therefore</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>45</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Solving <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, we have</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>41</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>But <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><msup><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>49</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>51</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>17</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q12. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let the equation of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> containing the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and the distance of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math>. Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **355**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p>The line  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> which passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Also given the equation of plane  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math> will lie on it as plane contains the line,</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Now product of direction ratio of line and direction ratio of plane will be zero as they both are perpendicular,</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Now on solving equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math> we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the equation of plane will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now distance from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>from plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>54</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>70</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>350</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>355</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q13. JEE Main 2023 (24 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> measure parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mstyle><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>13</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>31</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>26</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>26</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of line passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mstyle><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mstyle><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow><mn>12</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Any point on this line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p>For finding point of intersection of line and a plane, we have</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Required distance is </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msqrt><mn>36</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>576</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>26</mn></math> units</p>

</details>
