### Q33. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the equation of plane passing through the mirror image of a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> with respect to line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and containing the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math> is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>19</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>21</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>19</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The given line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo></math>let<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, any point on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> be the foot of perpendicular from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/bc108048-a9f7-45f1-bbc2-68cac41a7f6d-image.png" style="width: 385px; height: 197px;" /></p><p>Now, direction ratios of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>M</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>M</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then the sum of the product of their direction ratios is zero.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mi>&#955;</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>The mid-point of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> and the image of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> in the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Reflection <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, the plane passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Also, the plane contains the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> hence it passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and the normal to the plane is perpendicular to the line.</p><p>The equation of a plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and having normal perpendicular to a line with direction ratios <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>&#62;</mo></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Thus, the required plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn></math></p><p>Given the plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn></math></p><p><math ### Q35. JEE Main 2021 (16 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> containing the line, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>. If plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> divides the line segment <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> joining points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>**

style="margin:0cm 0cm 8pt">Plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p style="margin:0cm 0cm 8pt"><math ### Q36. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

The vector equation of the plane passing through the intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></math> and the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math ### Q37. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the point of intersection of the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>19</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>19</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>38</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>38</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>38</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p>For a point of intersection with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math>, then it satisfies the equation of a plane. </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> The point of intersection is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Then, distance between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>38</mn></msqrt></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q38. JEE Main 2020 (06 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> meets the coordinate axes at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> respectively. The centroid of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>ABC</mi></math> is given to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then the equation of the line through this centroid and perpendicular to the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>G</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#947;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3e137640-b28b-48e2-95bb-125602b51e9e-image.png" style="width: 298px; height: 240px;" />  </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math></p><p>eequation of plane is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math></p><p>required line</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q39. JEE Main 2020 (05 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If for some,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> are coplanar, then the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> passes through the point :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Lines are coplanar
so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>
Now by cross checking option (1) is correct.

</details>

### Q40. JEE Main 2020 (04 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> measured parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation PQ <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math>
Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> lies on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>15</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>PQ</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>15</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e624d138-9f05-4d3b-8527-14cdf1ffa434-image.png" style="width: 273px; height: 155px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q41. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> contain two lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the foot of the perpendicular drawn from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> equals .......

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Normal vector to the plane containing to given lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Direction ratios of normal vector <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.</p><p>Equation of plane will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is foot of perpendicular drawn from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane, then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>.</p>

### Q42. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>l</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Do not intersect for any values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math>
- **(B)** Intersect for all values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math>
- **(C)** Intersect when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** Intersect when <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Do not intersect for any values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>l</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Clearly both lines do not have same direction means lines are not parallel.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>Lines are skew lines or intersecting lines.</p><p>Let lines are skew lines and distance between lines is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi></math> if distance between them comes out zero then they will be intersecting lines.</p><p>For lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math></p><p>Applying here</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>Lines are skew lines means do not intersect.</p>

### Q43. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the distance between the plane, <math><mn>23</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the plane containing the lines <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>633</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to ____________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Lines must be intersecting</p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>6</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#955;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>s</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p>Distance of plane contains given lines from given plane is same as distance between point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from given plane.</p><p>Required distance equal to</p><p><math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>69</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>529</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>633</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>633</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q44. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The length of the perpendicular drawn from the point <math><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> to the plane containing the lines <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>i</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>j</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  
Equation of plane 
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><msub><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>|</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q45. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> intersects the plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> at a point <math><mi>P</mi></math> and the plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math> at a point <math><mi>Q</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>14</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math>  
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
Intersection with plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
Hence, <math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mn>13</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>  
Intersection with plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math>  
Hence, <math><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math>  
<math><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mi>Q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>56</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q46. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the line, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math> meets the plane, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math> at a point <math><mi>P</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the distance of <math><mi>P</mi></math> from the origin is,

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Let, coordinate of any point on line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is intersection point of plane &amp; line then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> will lie on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>The distance of a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> from origin <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><msup><mfenced><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>81</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></math>units.</p>

</details>

### Q48. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The perpendicular distance from the origin to the plane containing the two lines, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>11</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>11</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>11</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Direction vector of normal to the plane <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Hence, direction ratio of normal to the plane <math><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math></p><p>Thus, equation of plane is <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, perpendicular distance from origin <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>11</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q50. JEE Main 2019 (11 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The plane containing the line $\frac{x-3}{2}=\frac{y+2}{-1}=\frac{z-1}{3}$ and also containing its projection on the plane $2 \mathrm{x}+3 \mathrm{y}-\mathrm{z}=5$, contains which one of the following points?

**Options:**

- **(A)** (2,2,0)
- **(B)** (-2,2,2)
- **(C)** (0,-2,2)
- **(D)** (2,0,-2)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) (2,0,-2)**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let normal to the required plane is $\vec{n}$

$\Rightarrow \vec{n}$ is perpendicular to both vector $2 \hat{i}-\hat{j}+3 \hat{k}$ and

$2 \hat{i}+3 \hat{j}-3 \hat{k}$

$\Rightarrow \quad \vec{n}=\left|\begin{array}{ccc}\hat{i} & \hat{j} & \hat{k} \\ 2 & -1 & 3 \\ 2 & 3 & -1\end{array}\right|=-8 \hat{i}+8 \hat{j}+8 \hat{k}$

$\Rightarrow$ Equation of the required plane is $\Rightarrow \quad(x-3)(-8)+(y+2) \times 8+(z-1) \times 8=0$

$\Rightarrow \quad(x-3)(-1)+(y+2) \times 1+(z-1) \times 1=0$

$\Rightarrow x-3-y-2-z+1=0$

$\because \quad x-y-z=4$ passes through (2,0,-2)

$\therefore \quad$ plane contains (2,0,-2)

</details>

---

### Q51. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

On which of the following lines lies the point of intersection of the line, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and the plane, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

A general point on the given line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> can we taken as <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

If it also lies on the given plane then

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Point of intersection is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> , which lies on <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q52. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The equation of the plane containing the straight line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math> and perpendicular to the plane containing the straight lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

### Q53. JEE Main 2017 (09 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the line, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> lies in the plane, <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> , then the shortest distance between this line and the line, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/92139386-d982-4251-b852-fdd5fc608c4d-image.png" style="width: 306px; height: 200px;" /></p><p>The points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>-&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> lie on Plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

Now,

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>.......<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>i</mi></mfenced></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>........<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

Shortest distance formula

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></mfenced></mfrac></mfenced></math>

Calculating we get the value as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence lines are intersecting (As <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8742;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> )

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Shortest distance<math><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q54. JEE Main 2017 (08 Apr Online)

The coordinates of the foot of the perpendicular from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane containing the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>8</mn></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Direction ratio&#39;s of the plane perpendicular to two given lines is, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#215;</mo><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>&#160;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced><mrow><mn>49</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>40</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced><mrow><mn>42</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced><mrow><mn>30</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>d</mi><mi>r</mi><mo>&#39;</mo><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, plane passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> (A point on the first line),</p><p>Hence, the equation of plane containing lines is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>z</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Foot of perpendicular of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math
